## Narration 491

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-bukhari%3A4860)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Prophetic Commentary on the Qur&#x27;an (Tafseer of the Prophet (pbuh))</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنَا عَبْدُ اللَّهِ بْنُ مُحَمَّدٍ، أَخْبَرَنَا هِشَامُ بْنُ يُوسُفَ، أَخْبَرَنَا مَعْمَرٌ، عَنِ الزُّهْرِيِّ، عَنْ حُمَيْدِ بْنِ عَبْدِ الرَّحْمَنِ، عَنْ أَبِي هُرَيْرَةَ ـ رضى الله عنه ـ قَالَ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم ‏ &quot;‏ مَنْ حَلَفَ فَقَالَ فِي حَلِفِهِ وَاللاَّتِ وَالْعُزَّى‏.‏ فَلْيَقُلْ لاَ إِلَهَ إِلاَّ اللَّهُ‏.‏ وَمَنْ قَالَ لِصَاحِبِهِ تَعَالَ أُقَامِرْكَ‏.‏ فَلْيَتَصَدَّقْ ‏&quot;‏‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Sahih Al-Bukhari · 4860]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/bukhari/4860" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sahih Al-Bukhari</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Sahih Al-Bukhari · 4860</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Sahih Al-Bukhari   4860 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Sources credited by this publisher</summary>

<p><a href="https://github.com/fawazahmed0/hadith-api">Fawaz Ahmed Hadith API</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

<p><a href="https://sunnah.com/">Sunnah.com</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Narrated Abu Huraira: Allah&#x27;s Messenger (peace and blessings of Allah be upon him) said, &quot;Whomever takes an oath in which he mentions Lat and `Uzza (forgetfully), should say: None has the right to be worshipped but Allah, and whoever says to his companion. &#x27;Come along, let us gamble&#x27; must give alms (as an expiation)</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Sahih Al-Bukhari · 4860]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/bukhari/4860" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sahih Al-Bukhari</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>English · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Sahih Al-Bukhari · 4860</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Sahih Al-Bukhari   4860 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Sources credited by this publisher</summary>

<p><a href="https://github.com/fawazahmed0/hadith-api">Fawaz Ahmed Hadith API</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

<p><a href="https://sunnah.com/">Sunnah.com</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">हम से अब्दुल्लाह बिन मुहम्मद मुसनदी ने बयान किया, उन्होंने कहा हम को हिशाम बिन यूसुफ़ ने ख़बर दी, उन्होंने कहा हम को म&#x27;मर ने ख़बर दी, उन्होंने कहा हमें ज़ुहरी ने, उन्हें हुमैद बिन अब्दुर रहमान ने और उन से अबू हुरैराह (रदी अल्लाहु अन्हु) ने बयान किया के रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने फ़रमाया के जो शख़्स क़सम खाये और कहे के क़सम है लात और उज़्ज़ा की तो उसे तजदीद-ए-ईमान के लिए कहना चाहिये &#x27;ला इलाहा इल्लल्लाह&#x27; और जो शख़्स अपने साथी से यह कहे के आओ जुआ खेलें तो उसे सदक़ा देना चाहिये।</p>

<p dir="auto">[Sahih Al-Bukhari · 4860]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/bukhari/4860" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sahih Al-Bukhari</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="rtl">ہم سے عبداللہ بن محمد مسندی نے بیان کیا، انہوں نے کہا ہم کو ہشام بن یوسف نے خبر دی، انہوں نے کہا ہم کو معمر نے خبر دی، انہوں نے کہا ہمیں زہری نے، انہیں حمید بن عبدالرحمٰن نے اور ان سے ابوہریرہ رضی اللہ عنہ نے بیان کیا کہ رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم نے فرمایا کہ جو شخص قسم کھائے اور کہے کہ قسم ہے لات اور عزیٰ کی تو اسے تجدید ایمان کے لیے کہنا چاہئے «لا إله إلا الله» اور جو شخص اپنے ساتھی سے یہ کہے کہ آؤ جوا کھیلیں تو اسے صدقہ دینا چاہئے۔</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Sahih Al-Bukhari · 4860]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/bukhari/4860" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sahih Al-Bukhari</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur-Latn">
<h3>Roman Urdu</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Hum se Abdullah bin Muhammad Musnadi ne bayan kiya, unhon ne kaha hum ko Hisham bin Yusuf ne khabar di, unhon ne kaha hum ko Ma&#x27;mar ne khabar di, unhon ne kaha hamein Zuhri ne, unhein Humaid bin Abdur Rahman ne aur un se Abu Huraira (Radi Allahu Anhu) ne bayan kiya ke Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne farmaya ke jo shakhs qasam khaye aur kahe ke qasam hai Laat aur Uzza ki to use tajdeed-e-imaan ke liye kehna chahiye &#x27;La ilaha illallah&#x27; aur jo shakhs apne saathi se yeh kahe ke aao jua khelein to use sadqa dena chahiye.</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Sahih Al-Bukhari · 4860]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/bukhari/4860" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sahih Al-Bukhari</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

## Narration 492

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-bukhari%3A4862)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Prophetic Commentary on the Qur&#x27;an (Tafseer of the Prophet (pbuh))</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنَا أَبُو مَعْمَرٍ، حَدَّثَنَا عَبْدُ الْوَارِثِ، حَدَّثَنَا أَيُّوبُ، عَنْ عِكْرِمَةَ، عَنِ ابْنِ عَبَّاسٍ ـ رضى الله عنهما ـ قَالَ سَجَدَ النَّبِيُّ صلى الله عليه وسلم بِالنَّجْمِ وَسَجَدَ مَعَهُ الْمُسْلِمُونَ وَالْمُشْرِكُونَ وَالْجِنُّ وَالإِنْسُ‏.‏ تَابَعَهُ ابْنُ طَهْمَانَ عَنْ أَيُّوبَ‏.‏ وَلَمْ يَذْكُرِ ابْنُ عُلَيَّةَ ابْنَ عَبَّاسٍ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Sahih Al-Bukhari · 4862]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/bukhari/4862" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sahih Al-Bukhari</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Sahih Al-Bukhari · 4862</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Sahih Al-Bukhari   4862 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Sources credited by this publisher</summary>

<p><a href="https://github.com/fawazahmed0/hadith-api">Fawaz Ahmed Hadith API</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

<p><a href="https://sunnah.com/">Sunnah.com</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Narrated Ibn `Abbas: The Prophet (peace and blessings of Allah be upon him) performed a prostration when he finished reciting Surat-an-Najm, and all the Muslims and pagans and Jinns and human beings prostrated along with him</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Sahih Al-Bukhari · 4862]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/bukhari/4862" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sahih Al-Bukhari</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>English · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Sahih Al-Bukhari · 4862</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Sahih Al-Bukhari   4862 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Sources credited by this publisher</summary>

<p><a href="https://github.com/fawazahmed0/hadith-api">Fawaz Ahmed Hadith API</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

<p><a href="https://sunnah.com/">Sunnah.com</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">हम से अबू म&#x27;मर अब्दुल्लाह बिन अम्र ने बयान किया, उन से अब्दुल वारिस बिन सईद ने बयान किया, कहा हम से अय्यूब सख़्तियानी ने बयान किया, उन से इक्रमा ने और उन से इब्न अब्बास (रदी अल्लाहु अन्हुमा) ने बयान किया के नबी करीम (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने सूरह अन-नज्म में सज्दा किया और आप के साथ मुसलमानों ने और तमाम मुशरिकों और जिन्नात ओ इंसानों ने भी सज्दा किया। अब्दुल वारिस के साथ इस हदीस को इब्राहिम बिन तहमान ने भी अय्यूब से रिवायत किया और इस्मा&#x27;ईल बिन उलय्या ने अपनी रिवायत में इब्न अब्बास (रदी अल्लाहु अन्हुमा) का ज़िक्र नहीं किया।</p>

<p dir="auto">[Sahih Al-Bukhari · 4862]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/bukhari/4862" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sahih Al-Bukhari</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Hindi · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Sahih Al-Bukhari · 4862</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Sahih Al-Bukhari   4862 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Sources credited by this publisher</summary>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

<p><a href="https://sunnah.com/">Sunnah.com</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="rtl">ہم سے ابومعمر عبداللہ بن عمرو نے بیان کیا، ان سے عبدالوارث بن سعید نے بیان کیا، کہا ہم سے ایوب سختیانی نے بیان کیا، ان سے عکرمہ نے اور ان سے ابن عباس رضی اللہ عنہما نے بیان کیا کہ نبی کریم صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم نے سورۃ النجم میں سجدہ کیا اور آپ کے ساتھ مسلمانوں نے اور تمام مشرکوں اور جنات و انسانوں نے بھی سجدہ کیا۔ عبدالوارث کے ساتھ اس حدیث کو ابراہیم بن طہمان نے بھی ایوب سے روایت کیا اور اسمٰعیل بن علیہ نے اپنی روایت میں ابن عباس رضی اللہ عنہما کا ذکر نہیں کیا۔</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Sahih Al-Bukhari · 4862]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/bukhari/4862" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sahih Al-Bukhari</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur-Latn">
<h3>Roman Urdu</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Hum se Abu Ma&#x27;mar Abdullah bin Amr ne bayan kiya, un se Abdul Waris bin Saeed ne bayan kiya, kaha hum se Ayyub Sakhtiyani ne bayan kiya, un se Ikrama ne aur un se Ibn Abbas (Radi Allahu Anhuma) ne bayan kiya ke Nabi Kareem (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne Surah An-Najm mein sajda kiya aur aap ke sath musalmanon ne aur tamam mushrikon aur jinnat o insanon ne bhi sajda kiya. Abdul Waris ke sath is hadith ko Ibrahim bin Tahman ne bhi Ayyub se riwayat kiya aur Isma&#x27;il bin Ulayya ne apni riwayat mein Ibn Abbas (Radi Allahu Anhuma) ka zikr nahi kiya.</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Sahih Al-Bukhari · 4862]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/bukhari/4862" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sahih Al-Bukhari</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

## Narration 493

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-bukhari%3A4863)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Prophetic Commentary on the Qur&#x27;an (Tafseer of the Prophet (pbuh))</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنَا نَصْرُ بْنُ عَلِيٍّ، أَخْبَرَنِي أَبُو أَحْمَدَ، حَدَّثَنَا إِسْرَائِيلُ، عَنْ أَبِي إِسْحَاقَ، عَنِ الأَسْوَدِ بْنِ يَزِيدَ، عَنْ عَبْدِ اللَّهِ ـ رضى الله عنه ـ قَالَ أَوَّلُ سُورَةٍ أُنْزِلَتْ فِيهَا سَجْدَةٌ ‏{‏وَالنَّجْمِ‏}‏‏.‏ قَالَ فَسَجَدَ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم وَسَجَدَ مَنْ خَلْفَهُ، إِلاَّ رَجُلاً رَأَيْتُهُ أَخَذَ كَفًّا مِنْ تُرَابٍ فَسَجَدَ عَلَيْهِ، فَرَأَيْتُهُ بَعْدَ ذَلِكَ قُتِلَ كَافِرًا، وَهْوَ أُمَيَّةُ بْنُ خَلَفٍ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Sahih Al-Bukhari · 4863]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/bukhari/4863" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sahih Al-Bukhari</a></p>

<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p dir="auto">[ Sahih Al-Bukhari   4863 ]</p>

</details>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Narrated `Abdullah: The first Sura in which a prostration was mentioned, was Sura An-Najm (The Star). Allah&#x27;s Messenger (peace and blessings of Allah be upon him) prostrated (while reciting it), and everybody behind him prostrated except a man whom I saw taking a hand-full of dust in his hand and prostrated on it. Later I saw that man killed as an infidel, and he was Umaiya bin Khalaf</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Sahih Al-Bukhari · 4863]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/bukhari/4863" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sahih Al-Bukhari</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<p><a href="https://sunnah.com/">Sunnah.com</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">हम से नस्र बिन अली ने बयान किया, कहा हम को अबू अहमद ज़ुबैरी ने ख़बर दी, कहा हम को इस्रा&#x27;इल ने ख़बर दी, उन से अबू इशाक़ ने, उन से अस्वद बिन यज़ीद ने और उन से अब्दुल्लाह बिन मस&#x27;ऊद (रदी अल्लाहु अन्हु) ने बयान किया के सब से पहले नाज़िल होने वाली सज्दा वाली सूरह सूरह अन-नज्म है। बयान किया के फिर रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने (इस की तिलावत के बाद) सज्दा किया और जितने लोग आप के पीछे थे सब ही ने आप (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) के साथ सज्दा किया, सिवा एक शख़्स के, मैं ने देखा के उस ने अपनी हथेली में मिट्टी उठायी और उसी पर सज्दा कर लिया। बाद में (बद्र की लड़ाई में) मैं ने उसे देखा के कुफ़्र की हालत में वह क़त्ल किया हुआ पड़ा है। वह शख़्स उमय्या बिन ख़लफ़ था।</p>

<p dir="auto">[Sahih Al-Bukhari · 4863]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/bukhari/4863" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sahih Al-Bukhari</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="rtl">ہم سے نصر بن علی نے بیان کیا، کہا ہم کو ابواحمد زبیری نے خبر دی، کہا ہم کو اسرائیل نے خبر دی، ان سے ابواسحاق نے، ان سے اسود بن یزید نے اور ان سے عبداللہ بن مسعود رضی اللہ عنہ نے بیان کیا کہ سب سے پہلے نازل ہونے والی سجدہ والی سورت سورۃ النجم ہے۔ بیان کیا کہ پھر رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم نے ( اس کی تلاوت کے بعد ) سجدہ کیا اور جتنے لوگ آپ کے پیچھے تھے سب ہی نے آپ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم کے ساتھ سجدہ کیا، سوا ایک شخص کے، میں نے دیکھا کہ اس نے اپنی ہتھیلی میں مٹی اٹھائی اور اسی پر سجدہ کر لیا۔ بعد میں ( بدر کی لڑائی میں ) میں نے اسے دیکھا کہ کفر کی حالت میں وہ قتل کیا ہوا پڑا ہے۔ وہ شخص امیہ بن خلف تھا۔</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Sahih Al-Bukhari · 4863]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/bukhari/4863" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sahih Al-Bukhari</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur-Latn">
<h3>Roman Urdu</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Hum se Nasr bin Ali ne bayan kiya, kaha hum ko Abu Ahmad Zubairi ne khabar di, kaha hum ko Isra&#x27;il ne khabar di, un se Abu Ishaq ne, un se Aswad bin Yazid ne aur un se Abdullah bin Mas&#x27;ud (Radi Allahu Anhu) ne bayan kiya ke sab se pehle nazil hone wali sajda wali surah Surah An-Najm hai. Bayan kiya ke phir Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne (is ki tilawat ke baad) sajda kiya aur jitne log aap ke peeche the sab hi ne aap (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ke sath sajda kiya, siwa ek shakhs ke, main ne dekha ke us ne apni hatheli mein mitti uthayi aur usi par sajda kar liya. Baad mein (Badr ki ladai mein) main ne use dekha ke kufr ki halat mein woh qatl kiya hua pada hai. Woh shakhs Umayya bin Khalaf tha.</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Sahih Al-Bukhari · 4863]</p>

## Narration 494

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-bukhari%3A4864)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Prophetic Commentary on the Qur&#x27;an (Tafseer of the Prophet (pbuh))</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنَا مُسَدَّدٌ، حَدَّثَنَا يَحْيَى، عَنْ شُعْبَةَ، وَسُفْيَانَ، عَنِ الأَعْمَشِ، عَنْ إِبْرَاهِيمَ، عَنْ أَبِي مَعْمَرٍ، عَنِ ابْنِ مَسْعُودٍ، قَالَ انْشَقَّ الْقَمَرُ عَلَى عَهْدِ رَسُولِ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم فِرْقَتَيْنِ، فِرْقَةً فَوْقَ الْجَبَلِ وَفِرْقَةً دُونَهُ فَقَالَ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم ‏ &quot;‏ اشْهَدُوا ‏&quot;‏‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Sahih Al-Bukhari · 4864]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/bukhari/4864" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sahih Al-Bukhari</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Sahih Al-Bukhari · 4864</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Sahih Al-Bukhari   4864 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Sources credited by this publisher</summary>

<p><a href="https://github.com/fawazahmed0/hadith-api">Fawaz Ahmed Hadith API</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

<p><a href="https://sunnah.com/">Sunnah.com</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Narrated Ibn Masud: During the lifetime of Allah&#x27;s Messenger (peace and blessings of Allah be upon him) the moon was split into two parts; one part remained over the mountain, and the other part went beyond the mountain. On that, Allah&#x27;s Messenger (peace and blessings of Allah be upon him) said, &quot;Witness this miracle</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Sahih Al-Bukhari · 4864]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/bukhari/4864" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sahih Al-Bukhari</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>English · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Sahih Al-Bukhari · 4864</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">हम से मुसद्दद ने बयान किया, कहा हम से यह्या ने बयान किया, उन से शु&#x27;बा और सुफ़यान ने और उन से अ&#x27;मश ने, उन से इब्राहिम ने, उन से अबू म&#x27;मर ने और उन से अब्दुल्लाह बिन मस&#x27;ऊद (रदी अल्लाहु अन्हु) ने बयान किया के रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) के ज़माने में चाँद दो टुकड़े हो गया था एक टुकड़ा पहाड़ के ऊपर और दूसरा उस के पीछे चला गया था। नबी करीम (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने इसी मौक़े पर हम से फ़रमाया था के गवाह रहना।</p>

<p dir="auto">[Sahih Al-Bukhari · 4864]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/bukhari/4864" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sahih Al-Bukhari</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="rtl">ہم سے مسدد نے بیان کیا، کہا ہم سے یحییٰ نے بیان کیا، ان سے شعبہ اور سفیان نے اور ان سے اعمش نے، ان سے ابراہیم نے، ان سے ابومعمر نے اور ان سے عبداللہ بن مسعود رضی اللہ عنہ نے بیان کیا کہ رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم کے زمانے میں چاند دو ٹکڑے ہو گیا تھا ایک ٹکڑا پہاڑ کے اوپر اور دوسرا اس کے پیچھے چلا گیا تھا۔ نبی کریم صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم نے اسی موقع پر ہم سے فرمایا تھا کہ گواہ رہنا۔</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Sahih Al-Bukhari · 4864]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/bukhari/4864" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sahih Al-Bukhari</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur-Latn">
<h3>Roman Urdu</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Hum se Musaddad ne bayan kiya, kaha hum se Yahya ne bayan kiya, un se Shu&#x27;ba aur Sufyan ne aur un se A&#x27;mash ne, un se Ibrahim ne, un se Abu Ma&#x27;mar ne aur un se Abdullah bin Mas&#x27;ud (Radi Allahu Anhu) ne bayan kiya ke Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ke zamane mein chand do tukde ho gaya tha ek tukda pahad ke upar aur dusra us ke peeche chala gaya tha. Nabi Kareem (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne isi mauqe par hum se farmaya tha ke gawah rehna.</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Sahih Al-Bukhari · 4864]</p>

## Narration 495

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-bukhari%3A4866)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Prophetic Commentary on the Qur&#x27;an (Tafseer of the Prophet (pbuh))</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنَا يَحْيَى بْنُ بُكَيْرٍ، قَالَ حَدَّثَنِي بَكْرٌ، عَنْ جَعْفَرٍ، عَنْ عِرَاكِ بْنِ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ عُبَيْدِ اللَّهِ بْنِ عَبْدِ اللَّهِ بْنِ عُتْبَةَ بْنِ مَسْعُودٍ، عَنِ ابْنِ عَبَّاسٍ ـ رضى الله عنهما ـ قَالَ انْشَقَّ الْقَمَرُ فِي زَمَانِ النَّبِيِّ صلى الله عليه وسلم‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Sahih Al-Bukhari · 4866]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/bukhari/4866" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sahih Al-Bukhari</a></p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">हम से यह्या बिन बुकैर ने बयान किया, कहा के मुझ से बक्र ने बयान किया, उन से जफ़र ने, उन से अरक बिन मालिक ने, उन से उबैदुल्लाह बिन अब्दुल्लाह बिन उत्बा बिन मस&#x27;ऊद ने बयान किया के अब्दुल्लाह बिन अब्बास (रदी अल्लाहु अन्हुमा) ने बयान किया के नबी करीम (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) के ज़माने में चाँद फट गया था।</p>

<p dir="auto">[Sahih Al-Bukhari · 4866]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/bukhari/4866" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sahih Al-Bukhari</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="rtl">ہم سے یحییٰ بن بکیر نے بیان کیا، کہا کہ مجھ سے بکر نے بیان کیا، ان سے جعفر نے، ان سے عراک بن مالک نے، ان سے عبیداللہ بن عبداللہ بن عتبہ بن مسعود نے بیان کیا کہ عبداللہ بن عباس رضی اللہ عنہما نے بیان کیا کہ نبی کریم صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم کے زمانے میں چاند پھٹ گیا تھا۔</p>

## Narration 496

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-bukhari%3A4869)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Prophetic Commentary on the Qur&#x27;an (Tafseer of the Prophet (pbuh))</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنَا حَفْصُ بْنُ عُمَرَ، حَدَّثَنَا شُعْبَةُ، عَنْ أَبِي إِسْحَاقَ، عَنِ الأَسْوَدِ، عَنْ عَبْدِ اللَّهِ، قَالَ كَانَ النَّبِيُّ صلى الله عليه وسلم يَقْرَأُ ‏{‏فَهَلْ مِنْ مُدَّكِرٍ‏}‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Sahih Al-Bukhari · 4869]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/bukhari/4869" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sahih Al-Bukhari</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Sahih Al-Bukhari · 4869</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Sahih Al-Bukhari   4869 ]</p>

</details>

<summary>Sources credited by this publisher</summary>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

<p><a href="https://sunnah.com/">Sunnah.com</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Narrated `Abdullah bin Masud: The Prophet (peace and blessings of Allah be upon him) used to recite: &quot;Fahal-min-Maddakir (then is there any that will receive admonition?)</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Sahih Al-Bukhari · 4869]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/bukhari/4869" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sahih Al-Bukhari</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<p><a href="https://sunnah.com/">Sunnah.com</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">हम से हफ़्स बिन उमर ने बयान किया, कहा हम से शु&#x27;बा ने बयान किया, उन से अबू इशाक़ ने, उन से अस्वद ने और उन से अब्दुल्लाह बिन मस&#x27;ऊद (रदी अल्लाहु अन्हु) ने बयान किया के नबी करीम (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) &#x27;फ़हल मिन मुद्दकिर&#x27; पढ़ा करते थे।</p>

<p dir="auto">[Sahih Al-Bukhari · 4869]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/bukhari/4869" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sahih Al-Bukhari</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Sahih Al-Bukhari   4869 ]</p>

<p><a href="https://sunnah.com/">Sunnah.com</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="rtl">ہم سے حفص بن عمر نے بیان کیا، کہا ہم سے شعبہ نے بیان کیا، ان سے ابواسحاق نے، ان سے اسود نے اور ان سے عبداللہ بن مسعود رضی اللہ عنہ نے بیان کیا کہ نبی کریم صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم «فهل من مدكر‏» پڑھا کرتے تھے۔</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Sahih Al-Bukhari · 4869]</p>

## Narration 497

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-bukhari%3A4870)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Prophetic Commentary on the Qur&#x27;an (Tafseer of the Prophet (pbuh))</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنَا مُسَدَّدٌ، عَنْ يَحْيَى، عَنْ شُعْبَةَ، عَنْ أَبِي إِسْحَاقَ، عَنِ الأَسْوَدِ، عَنْ عَبْدِ اللَّهِ ـ رضى الله عنه ـ عَنِ النَّبِيِّ صلى الله عليه وسلم‏.‏ أَنَّهُ كَانَ يَقْرَأُ ‏{‏فَهَلْ مِنْ مُدَّكِرٍ‏}‏‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Sahih Al-Bukhari · 4870]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/bukhari/4870" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sahih Al-Bukhari</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Sahih Al-Bukhari · 4870</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Sahih Al-Bukhari   4870 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Sources credited by this publisher</summary>

<p><a href="https://github.com/fawazahmed0/hadith-api">Fawaz Ahmed Hadith API</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

<p><a href="https://sunnah.com/">Sunnah.com</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Narrated `Abdullah bin Masud: The Prophet (peace and blessings of Allah be upon him) used to recite: &quot;Fahal-min-Maddakir (then is there any that will receive admonition?)</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Sahih Al-Bukhari · 4870]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/bukhari/4870" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sahih Al-Bukhari</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>English · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Sahih Al-Bukhari · 4870</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Sahih Al-Bukhari   4870 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Sources credited by this publisher</summary>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

<p><a href="https://sunnah.com/">Sunnah.com</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">हम से मुसद्दद ने बयान किया, उन से यह्या बिन सईद क़त्तान ने बयान किया, उन से शु&#x27;बा ने, उन से अबू इशाक़ ने, उन से अस्वद ने और उन से अब्दुल्लाह बिन मस&#x27;ऊद (रदी अल्लाहु अन्हु) ने के नबी करीम (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) &#x27;फ़हल मिन मुद्दकिर&#x27; (सो है कोई नसीहत हासिल करने वाला?) पढ़ा करते थे।</p>

<p dir="auto">[Sahih Al-Bukhari · 4870]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/bukhari/4870" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sahih Al-Bukhari</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<summary>Hindi · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Sahih Al-Bukhari · 4870</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Sahih Al-Bukhari   4870 ]</p>

</details>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

<p><a href="https://sunnah.com/">Sunnah.com</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="rtl">ہم سے مسدد نے بیان کیا، ان سے یحییٰ بن سعید قطان نے بیان کیا، ان سے شعبہ نے، ان سے ابواسحاق نے، ان سے اسود نے اور ان سے عبداللہ بن مسعود رضی اللہ عنہ نے کہ نبی کریم صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم «فهل من مدكر‏» ( سو ہے کوئی نصیحت حاصل کرنے والا؟ ) پڑھا کرتے تھے۔</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Sahih Al-Bukhari · 4870]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/bukhari/4870" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sahih Al-Bukhari</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur-Latn">
<h3>Roman Urdu</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Hum se Musaddad ne bayan kiya, un se Yahya bin Saeed Qattan ne bayan kiya, un se Shu&#x27;ba ne, un se Abu Ishaq ne, un se Aswad ne aur un se Abdullah bin Mas&#x27;ud (Radi Allahu Anhu) ne ke Nabi Kareem (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) &#x27;Fahal min muddakir&#x27; (so hai koi nasihat hasil karne wala?) parha karte the.</p>

## Narration 498

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-bukhari%3A4871)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Prophetic Commentary on the Qur&#x27;an (Tafseer of the Prophet (pbuh))</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنَا أَبُو نُعَيْمٍ، حَدَّثَنَا زُهَيْرٌ، عَنْ أَبِي إِسْحَاقَ، أَنَّهُ سَمِعَ رَجُلاً، سَأَلَ الأَسْوَدَ فَهَلْ مِنْ مُدَّكِرٍ أَوْ مُذَّكِرٍ فَقَالَ سَمِعْتُ عَبْدَ اللَّهِ يَقْرَؤُهَا ‏{‏فَهَلْ مِنْ مُدَّكِرٍ‏}‏ قَالَ وَسَمِعْتُ النَّبِيَّ صلى الله عليه وسلم يَقْرَؤُهَا ‏{‏فَهَلْ مِنْ مُدَّكِرٍ‏}‏ دَالاً‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Sahih Al-Bukhari · 4871]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/bukhari/4871" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sahih Al-Bukhari</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Sahih Al-Bukhari · 4871</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Sahih Al-Bukhari   4871 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Sources credited by this publisher</summary>

<p><a href="https://github.com/fawazahmed0/hadith-api">Fawaz Ahmed Hadith API</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

<p><a href="https://sunnah.com/">Sunnah.com</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Narrated Abu &#x27;Is-haq: A man asked Al-Aswad, &#x27;is it &#x27;Fahal min-Muddakir&#x27; or&#x27;..Mudhdhakir?&quot; Al Aswad replied, &#x27;I have heard `Abdullah bin Masud reciting it, &#x27;Fahal-min Muddakir&#x27;; I too, heard the Prophet (peace and blessings of Allah be upon him) reciting it &#x27;Fahal-min-Muddakir&#x27; with &#x27;d</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Sahih Al-Bukhari · 4871]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/bukhari/4871" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sahih Al-Bukhari</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>English · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Sahih Al-Bukhari · 4871</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Sahih Al-Bukhari   4871 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Sources credited by this publisher</summary>

<p><a href="https://github.com/fawazahmed0/hadith-api">Fawaz Ahmed Hadith API</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

<p><a href="https://sunnah.com/">Sunnah.com</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">हम से अबू नु&#x27;ऐम ने बयान किया, कहा हम से ज़ुहैर ने बयान किया, उन से अबू इशाक़ ने, उन्होंने एक शख़्स को अस्वद से पूछते सुना के सूरह अल-क़मर में आयत &#x27;फ़हल मिन मुद्दकिर&#x27; है या &#x27;फ़हल मिन मुधक्किर&#x27;? उन्होंने कहा के मैं ने इब्न मस&#x27;ऊद (रदी अल्लाहु अन्हु) से सुना वह &#x27;फ़हल मिन मुद्दकिर&#x27; पढ़ते थे। उन्होंने कहा के मैं ने नबी करीम (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) को भी &#x27;फ़हल मिन मुद्दकिर&#x27; पढ़ते सुना है (दाल मुहमला से)।</p>

<p dir="auto">[Sahih Al-Bukhari · 4871]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/bukhari/4871" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sahih Al-Bukhari</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Hindi · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Sahih Al-Bukhari · 4871</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Sahih Al-Bukhari   4871 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Sources credited by this publisher</summary>

<p><a href="https://github.com/fawazahmed0/hadith-api">Fawaz Ahmed Hadith API</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

<p><a href="https://sunnah.com/">Sunnah.com</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="rtl">ہم سے ابونعیم نے بیان کیا، کہا ہم سے زہیر نے بیان کیا، ان سے ابواسحاق نے انہوں نے ایک شخص کو اسود سے پوچھتے سنا کہ سورۃ القمر میں آیت «فهل من مدكر‏» ہے یا «فهل من مذكر‏» ؟ انہوں نے کہا کہ میں نے ابن مسعود رضی اللہ عنہ سے سنا وہ «فهل من مدكر‏» پڑھتے تھے۔ انہوں نے کہا کہ میں نے نبی کریم صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم کو بھی «فهل من مدكر‏» پڑھتے سنا ہے ( دال مہملہ سے ) ۔</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Sahih Al-Bukhari · 4871]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/bukhari/4871" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sahih Al-Bukhari</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur-Latn">
<h3>Roman Urdu</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Hum se Abu Nu&#x27;aim ne bayan kiya, kaha hum se Zuhair ne bayan kiya, un se Abu Ishaq ne, unhon ne ek shakhs ko Aswad se puchte suna ke Surah Al-Qamar mein aayat &#x27;Fahal min muddakir&#x27; hai ya &#x27;Fahal min mudhakkir&#x27;? Unhon ne kaha ke main ne Ibn Mas&#x27;ud (Radi Allahu Anhu) se suna woh &#x27;Fahal min muddakir&#x27; parhte the. Unhon ne kaha ke main ne Nabi Kareem (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ko bhi &#x27;Fahal min muddakir&#x27; parhte suna hai (Daal muhmala se).</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Sahih Al-Bukhari · 4871]</p>

## Narration 499

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-bukhari%3A4872)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Prophetic Commentary on the Qur&#x27;an (Tafseer of the Prophet (pbuh))</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنَا عَبْدَانُ، أَخْبَرَنَا أَبِي، عَنْ شُعْبَةَ، عَنْ أَبِي إِسْحَاقَ، عَنِ الأَسْوَدِ، عَنْ عَبْدِ اللَّهِ ـ رضى الله عنه ـ عَنِ النَّبِيِّ صلى الله عليه وسلم قَرَأَ ‏{‏فَهَلْ مِنْ مُدَّكِرٍ‏}‏ الآيَةَ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Sahih Al-Bukhari · 4872]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/bukhari/4872" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sahih Al-Bukhari</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Sahih Al-Bukhari · 4872</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Sahih Al-Bukhari   4872 ]</p>

</details>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

<p><a href="https://sunnah.com/">Sunnah.com</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Narrated `Abdullah bin Masud: The Prophet (peace and blessings of Allah be upon him) used to recite: &quot;Fahal-min-Maddakir (then is there any that will receive admonition?)</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Sahih Al-Bukhari · 4872]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/bukhari/4872" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sahih Al-Bukhari</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<p><a href="https://sunnah.com/">Sunnah.com</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">हम से अबदान ने बयान किया, कहा हम को हमारे वालिद उस्मान ने ख़बर दी, उन्हें शु&#x27;बा ने, उन्हें अबी इशाक़ ने, उन्हें अस्वद ने और उन्हें इब्न मस&#x27;ऊद (रदी अल्लाहु अन्हु) ने के नबी करीम (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने &#x27;फ़हल मिन मुद्दकिर&#x27; अल-आयह (दाल मुहमला से)।</p>

<p dir="auto">[Sahih Al-Bukhari · 4872]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/bukhari/4872" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sahih Al-Bukhari</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="rtl">ہم سے عبدان نے بیان کیا، کہا ہم کو ہمارے والد عثمان نے خبر دی، انہیں شعبہ نے، انہیں ابی اسحاق نے، انہیں اسود نے اور انہیں ابن مسعود رضی اللہ عنہ نے کہ نبی کریم صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم نے «فهل من مدكر‏» الآیۃ ( دال مہملہ سے ) ۔</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Sahih Al-Bukhari · 4872]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/bukhari/4872" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sahih Al-Bukhari</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur-Latn">
<h3>Roman Urdu</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Hum se Abdan ne bayan kiya, kaha hum ko hamare walid Usman ne khabar di, unhein Shu&#x27;ba ne, unhein Abi Ishaq ne, unhein Aswad ne aur unhein Ibn Mas&#x27;ud (Radi Allahu Anhu) ne ke Nabi Kareem (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne &#x27;Fahal min muddakir&#x27; al-ayah (Daal muhmala se).</p>

## Narration 500

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-bukhari%3A4873)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Prophetic Commentary on the Qur&#x27;an (Tafseer of the Prophet (pbuh))</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنَا مُحَمَّدٌ، حَدَّثَنَا غُنْدَرٌ، حَدَّثَنَا شُعْبَةُ، عَنْ أَبِي إِسْحَاقَ، عَنِ الأَسْوَدِ، عَنْ عَبْدِ اللَّهِ، عَنِ النَّبِيِّ صلى الله عليه وسلم قَرَأَ ‏{‏فَهَلْ مِنْ مُدَّكِرٍ‏، ولقد أهلكنا أشياعكم فهل من مُدَّكِرٍ‏}‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Sahih Al-Bukhari · 4873]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/bukhari/4873" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sahih Al-Bukhari</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Sahih Al-Bukhari · 4873</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Sahih Al-Bukhari   4873 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Sources credited by this publisher</summary>

<p><a href="https://github.com/fawazahmed0/hadith-api">Fawaz Ahmed Hadith API</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

<p><a href="https://sunnah.com/">Sunnah.com</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Narrated `Abdullah: The Prophet (peace and blessings of Allah be upon him) recited: &#x27;Fahal-min Muddakir&#x27;: &#x27;And verily, We have destroyed nations like unto you; then is there any that will receive admonition?</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Sahih Al-Bukhari · 4873]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/bukhari/4873" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sahih Al-Bukhari</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>English · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Sahih Al-Bukhari · 4873</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Sahih Al-Bukhari   4873 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Sources credited by this publisher</summary>

<p><a href="https://github.com/fawazahmed0/hadith-api">Fawaz Ahmed Hadith API</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

<p><a href="https://sunnah.com/">Sunnah.com</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">हम से मुहम्मद बिन बशर ने बयान किया, कहा हम से ग़ुन्दर ने बयान किया, कहा हम से शु&#x27;बा ने बयान किया, उन से अबू इशाक़ ने, उन से अस्वद ने और उन से अब्दुल्लाह बिन मस&#x27;ऊद (रदी अल्लाहु अन्हु) ने के रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने &#x27;फ़हल मिन मुद्दकिर&#x27; (दाल मुहमला से) पढ़ा था।</p>

<p dir="auto">[Sahih Al-Bukhari · 4873]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/bukhari/4873" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sahih Al-Bukhari</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<p><a href="https://sunnah.com/">Sunnah.com</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="rtl">ہم سے محمد بن بشار نے بیان کیا، کہا ہم سے غندر نے بیان کیا، کہا ہم سے شعبہ نے بیان کیا، ان سے ابواسحاق نے، ان سے اسود نے اور ان سے عبداللہ بن مسعود رضی اللہ عنہ نے کہ رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم نے «فهل من مدكر‏» ( دال مہملہ سے ) پڑھا تھا۔</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Sahih Al-Bukhari · 4873]</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur-Latn">
<h3>Roman Urdu</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Hum se Muhammad bin Bashar ne bayan kiya, kaha hum se Ghundar ne bayan kiya, kaha hum se Shu&#x27;ba ne bayan kiya, un se Abu Ishaq ne, un se Aswad ne aur un se Abdullah bin Mas&#x27;ud (Radi Allahu Anhu) ne ke Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne &#x27;Fahal min muddakir&#x27; (Daal muhmala se) parha tha.</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Sahih Al-Bukhari · 4873]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/bukhari/4873" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sahih Al-Bukhari</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>
